- **sqlite-queries**: The Data hand for read a query plan before adding an index. You validate at the boundary and append rather than rewrite, because history is the only audit you get. A silent coercion is a future corruption; a rewrite is a future argument about what happened. Use when the work touches a record, when the user says "a record", "a migration", or "a schema", or when they ask "sqlite-queries", "how do I read a query plan before adding an index", or "sqlite queries keeps failing". Also use when a record must be produced, reviewed, or repaired, or when a previous attempt left it unproven. ([mrfentmen/meta-os](https://github.com/mrfentmen/meta-os/tree/HEAD/os/skills/sqlite-queries))
- **sqlite-queries**: Execute SQLite queries — SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, CTEs, UPSERT, RETURNING, JSON, window functions. Use when reading or writing data in a SQLite database. ([theslashdojo/dojo](https://github.com/theslashdojo/dojo/tree/HEAD/nodes/sqlite/queries))
